================================================================================
OPEN HOLE #9 -- THE ZEROED +/-0.5 CLAMP AT ROM 0x0357C8 / 0x0357CC,
ITS ROUTINE (0x034EF4 -> 0x0353BC -> 0x0355B8) AND EVERY CONSUMER OF 0xFFFF7904
================================================================================
Date:          2026-11-27
Reference bin: rom/AE5L600L 20g rev 20.19c.bin   md5 92cae8275cd4f9b473a3a9e36efe6449
Stock bin:     rom/ae5l600l.bin                  md5 a8ea39d447f977e270e27ee670243c88
Method:        BLIND. Every listing below is scripts/mapping/fn_dump.py output (no
               labels); semantics were worked out from the bytes, then run in
               scripts/sh2e_emu.py against a float32 model that rounds every
               fadd/fsub/fmul/fdiv/fmac TOWARD ZERO, as the chip does
               (FPSCR.RM = 01; corrections item 106)
               (scripts/trace/emu_hole9_afl.py, 272,262 assertions, exit 0).
               Existing labels/docs were read only for the comparison section.
Status:        THIRD DECODE. A tracer wrote this file; a reviewer disputed five
               claims and one contradiction and listed six omissions. Each was
               re-decided here from bytes and the emulator, and the file and the
               script were repaired. What changed, and on what evidence, is in
               REVIEW HISTORY at the end.
EVIDENCE CLASS: bytes + emulated for the three entry functions, the helpers, the
               siblings, the whole 0x03580C chain and the direct consumers; bytes
               only where marked; the physical identity of every quantity is NOT
               established here.

HOW TO READ THE LISTINGS
  * Instruction lines are fn_dump lines verbatim. Text after the first ';' is
    fn_dump's mechanical annotation; text after '|' (or after ';' when fn_dump
    printed no annotation) is this file's comment.
  * fn_dump's ".word" pool echo lines are dropped; its ".long" pool lines are kept.
  * [0xFFFF7904] means "the float at that RAM address"; "byte [..]" a byte.
  * Compare senses used throughout (checked in the emulator):
        fcmp/gt FRm,FRn   T = (FRn > FRm)     false if either is NaN
        cmp/eq #imm,r0    T = (r0 == imm)
        tst Rm,Rn         T = ((Rn & Rm) == 0)
        bt / bt/s  branch when T=1;  bf / bf/s  branch when T=0
        bt/s, bf/s, jsr, bsr, bra, rts, jmp: the NEXT instruction (delay slot)
        executes before the transfer, whether or not a conditional is taken.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
SUMMARY (each statement is proven in the section named)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 1. 0x0355B8 is a proportional + accumulated-error + signed-step controller. Its
    result is clamped twice and stored to 0xFFFF7904:
        inner = clamp(total, [0xFFFF318C] - [0xFFFF78DC], [0xFFFF318C] + [0xFFFF78E0])
        [0xFFFF7904] = clamp(inner, lo = ROM 0x0357CC, hi = ROM 0x0357C8)
    Stock: lo/hi = -0.5/+0.5. Every flashed rev: 0.0/0.0, and 0x0BE56C(x, 0, 0)
    returns +0.0 for EVERY x (including NaN, Inf, -0.0).         [FUNCTION 0x0355B8]
 2. So in the car today 0xFFFF7904 is forced to +0.0 on every pass in which the
    enable byte 0xFFFF7912 is 1. Nothing else the routine computes changes:
    all 12 other outputs are bit-identical with either pair of bounds. [emulated, B]
 3. The store is skipped (0xFFFF7904 left alone) when byte 0xFFFF7912 != 1, and
    there are two other writers that the zeroed pair does NOT touch:
        0x034F72  init:    [7904] = [318C] + step/[CC0BC]        (step in +-0.01, 0)
        0x035046  reload:  [7904] = [790C]  when byte [0xFFFF6A28] == 1
    Both copy the checksum-protected value 0xFFFF318C (directly, or via 0xFFFF790C
    which is only ever written with [318C] at init).   [0x034F28, 0x034F94 sections]
 4. A SECOND zeroed pair exists that open-hole #9 does not mention:
    ROM 0x035FFC / 0x036000 (stock +0.5 / -0.5, tuned 0.0 / 0.0), in 0x035F48, which
    is the store to 0xFFFF318C used on every pass of 0x0359A4 and by the reload
    block. With it zeroed, every such store writes +0.0 to 0xFFFF318C, so writers
    0x034F72 and 0x035046 also produce 0.0 once 0xFFFF318C has been rewritten and
    the module re-initialised. (The only other stores to 0xFFFF318C are the reset
    0x0357E8 and the unclamped init rewrite in 0x035E88, PART 6.) [FUNCTION 0x035F48]
 5. Consumers of 0xFFFF7904 (all seven literal-pool readers decoded):
        0x0334C6  ADDED 1:1 into a sum "1.0 + ..." that is clamped to
                  [0xCBF3C]=0.85 .. [0xFFFF7BDC] and then 0.75 .. 1.25 and stored to
                  0xFFFF77BC; 0xFFFF77BC is copied / ramped into 0xFFFF77C8, which
                  has 18 direct readers.  <-- the only control-path consumer live
                  with this calibration. (0xFFFF77BC..77C8 have a second writer,
                  0x0332A2, which sets all four to 1.0 when byte [0xFFFF65F6] == 1.)
        0x03225A  copied to a stack slot that is read only when byte [0xCBBDF]==1;
                  that byte is 0 in stock and tuned -> dead path
        0x0359C6  low-pass filtered into 0xFFFF7944, the input of the routine that
                  steps 0xFFFF318C; 0xFFFF7944 is read again by 0x035DE4 and 0x035E2C
        0x06673A  copied to 0xFFFF9340; the copy is tested against the [318C] -/+
                  window edges in 0x06665E (byte 932C), 0x0668B4 (byte 932D) and
                  0x066904 (byte 932E)
        0x02453A, 0x05DCFE, 0x0A8932  scaled integer exports (u16 / u8 / u8)
 6. Net numeric effect of the zeroing on the control path: the additive term
    [0xFFFF7904] in the 0xFFFF77BC sum is 0.0 instead of a value that, in stock,
    lies inside [L - wlo, L + whi] (L = [0xFFFF318C]; wlo <= 0.07, whi <= 0.03 from
    the tables). ONCE INIT (0x035E88) HAS RUN, stock L is in [-0.028, +0.038] and
    0x0359A4 keeps |L| <= 0.06 from then on (at most one 0.0005 step per 157
    calls), so stock [7904] stays within -0.13 .. +0.09 and the +-0.5 bound is
    never reached. Before the first init nothing but the +-0.5 clamps bounds a
    stored L. In stock the pair is a safety limit; in the tune it is an off
    switch.                                      [PART 5.3; emu sections E3, G, I]
 7. NOT established here: what 0xFFFF63CC and 0xFFFF77BC/77C8 physically are. For
    0xFFFF43FC the bytes give its source -- the 16-bit word 0xFFFF4050 times
    5/65536, and that word is a copy of the peripheral register 0xFFFFF834
    (PART 7) -- but not which sensor is wired there. See NOT ESTABLISHED.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
THE POINTER TABLE AT ROM 0x063A88 (30 longs, identical in stock and tuned)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
All three entry functions load 0x00063A88 from a literal pool and address RAM as
"mov.l @(off,Rtab),r2 ; fmov.s @r2,.."  -- a table of POINTERS, not a stack frame
and not a struct of values. Every @(disp,r13)/@(disp,r14)/@(r0,r13)/@(r0,r14) in
the listings resolves through this map:

;   off   ROM addr    -> RAM          role established from bytes
;   +0    0x063A88  -> 0xFFFF78B8   setpoint the error is measured against (0x035218)
;   +4    0x063A8C  -> 0xFFFF78BC   weight K on the previous-sample term (0x035340)
;   +8    0x063A90  -> 0xFFFF78C0   signed step term, +/- table (0x035340)
;   +12   0x063A94  -> 0xFFFF78C4   proportional gain Kp (0x0353BC)
;   +16   0x063A98  -> 0xFFFF78C8   accumulator gain Ki (0x0353BC)
;   +20   0x063A9C  -> 0xFFFF78CC   P-term lower limit (0x0353BC)
;   +24   0x063AA0  -> 0xFFFF78D0   P-term upper limit (0x0353BC)
;   +28   0x063AA4  -> 0xFFFF78D4   lower-window candidate from table (0x0353BC)
;   +32   0x063AA8  -> 0xFFFF78D8   lower-window alternative: 0.0 or [0xCC108] (0x0353BC)
;   +36   0x063AAC  -> 0xFFFF78DC   lower window width = max(+28, +32) (0x0353BC)
;   +40   0x063AB0  -> 0xFFFF78E0   upper window width (0x0353BC)
;   +44   0x063AB4  -> 0xFFFF78E4   error E (0x0355B8)
;   +48   0x063AB8  -> 0xFFFF78E8   filtered and one-side-limited error e2 (0x0355B8)
;   +52   0x063ABC  -> 0xFFFF78EC   filtered error F (0x0355B8)
;   +56   0x063AC0  -> 0xFFFF78F0   lower limit lo1 for e2 (0x0355B8)
;   +60   0x063AC4  -> 0xFFFF78F4   upper limit hi1 for e2 (0x0355B8)
;   +64   0x063AC8  -> 0xFFFF78F8   P term (0x0355B8)
;   +68   0x063ACC  -> 0xFFFF78FC   last committed Ki*accumulator (0x0355B8)
;   +72   0x063AD0  -> 0xFFFF7900   unclamped total (0x0355B8)
;   +76   0x063AD4  -> 0xFFFF7904   OUTPUT (writers 0x034F72, 0x035046, 0x03573E)
;   +80   0x063AD8  -> 0xFFFF7908   error accumulator (0x0355B8; preloaded 0x034F84, 0x035070)
;   +84   0x063ADC  -> 0xFFFF790C   copy of [0xFFFF318C] taken at init (0x034F8A)
;   +88   0x063AE0  -> 0xFFFF78B4   filtered measurement (0x0351FA)
;   +92   0x063AE4  -> 0xFFFF792C   [0xFFFF78B4] of the previous pass (0x035752)
;   +96   0x063AE8  -> 0xFFFF7930   window lower edge = [318C] - [78DC] (0x0355B8)
;   +100  0x063AEC  -> 0xFFFF7934   window upper edge = [78E0] + [318C] (0x0355B8)
;   +104  0x063AF0  -> 0xFFFF7910   u16 count of consecutive enabled passes (0x034F94)
;   +108  0x063AF4  -> 0xFFFF7912   u8 enable flag (0x034F94)
;   +112  0x063AF8  -> 0xFFFF7913   u8 "measurement >= setpoint" hysteresis flag (0x0352DE)
;   +116  0x063AFC  -> 0xFFFF7941   u8 mode byte = byte [0xFFFF984D] (0x034F06)
;   +120  0x063B00  -> 0xFFFF318C   first entry of a SECOND table (base 0x063B00,
;                                   loaded at 0x0358A0 / 0x0359A4 / 0x035E88)

The literal 0x00063A88 occurs in exactly three pools (0x035148, 0x035484, 0x035798),
loaded at 0x034F2C, 0x034FD4, 0x03522A, 0x0352DE, 0x035340, 0x0353BC, 0x0355B8.
No ROM long points at 0x063AD4 (the +76 slot) directly.
The base 0xFFFF7940 / 0xFFFF7920 / 0xFFFF7928 / 0xFFFF7924 / 0xFFFF792C registers in
the listings are also literal-pool constants; their @(r0,Rn) forms are resolved
in the comments (e.g. 0xFFFF7920 - 112 = 0xFFFF78B0).

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
THE ZEROED BYTES
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
;   ROM addr    stock bytes   stock f32   every flashed rev (20.8 .. 20.19e)
;   0x0357C8    3F 00 00 00   +0.5        00 00 00 00  = +0.0   hi of the 0x035734 clamp
;   0x0357CC    BF 00 00 00   -0.5        00 00 00 00  = +0.0   lo of the 0x035734 clamp
;   0x035FFC    3F 00 00 00   +0.5        00 00 00 00  = +0.0   hi of the 0x035F66 clamp
;   0x036000    BF 00 00 00   -0.5        00 00 00 00  = +0.0   lo of the 0x035F66 clamp
These four single-byte edits are the ONLY differences between stock and 20.19c
anywhere in 0x034EC8..0x036007 (checked byte for byte; emu script section 0).
Every scalar, descriptor record, axis, data block and pointer table that the
functions run by the script read -- twenty ROM ranges, printed by section 0 --
is identical in stock, in 20.19c and in each of the 19 flashed bins under rom/
(20.8 .. 20.19e). In all 19 the four longs are 00000000 and 0x034EC8..0x036007 is
byte-identical to 20.19c. (All asserted by the script, not just stated.)

################################################################################
PART 1 -- THE THREE ENTRY FUNCTIONS
################################################################################

==== FUNCTION 0x034EF4 .. 0x034F28  (26 instructions, 1 caller) ====
Caller: jsr at 0x049ED0 inside 0x049CF0 (a 314-instruction dispatcher with 96
call sites; the very next call, 0x049ED6, is 0x03580C). Rate not established.

CONTRACT
  inputs    [0xFFFF63CC] f32, byte [0xFFFF984D]; everything its nine callees read
  output    none in registers
  effects   [0xFFFF7920] = [0xFFFF63CC]
            byte [0xFFFF7941] = byte [0xFFFF984D]
            then, in this order: 0x034F94, 0x0351FA, 0x03528A, 0x035218, 0x0352DE,
            0x035318, 0x035340, 0x0353BC (bsr), 0x0355B8 (tail bra)

MECHANISM
  [0xFFFF7920] = [0xFFFF63CC]              ; base 0xFFFF7940, index -32
  byte [0xFFFF7941] = byte [0xFFFF984D]    ; stored in the delay slot of the first bsr
  call the eight helpers; restore PR; jump to 0x0355B8
  The function owns no arithmetic. 0x0355B8 returns to 0x034EF4's caller.

EVIDENCE
  0x034EFA..0x034EFE: r5 is the literal 0xFFFF7940, r0 = -32 -> 0xFFFF7920.
  0x034F06 is the delay slot of 0x034F04, so the byte store happens before
  0x034F94 runs; 0x034F94 does not read 0xFFFF7941.
  0x034F24/0x034F26: bra with lds.l @r15+,pr in the slot = tail call.
  emulated: emu_hole9_afl.py section D1 (both stores, all nine callee entry
  addresses executed, return to caller, r15 balanced, no uninitialised read).

LISTING

034EF8: F828  fmov.s @r2,fr8                ; fr8 = [0xFFFF63CC]

034EFC: E0E0  mov #-32,r0

==== FUNCTION 0x0353BC .. 0x0355B8  (161 instructions, 1 caller) ====
Caller: bsr at 0x034F20. Ends at the rts 0x0355B4 (+ slot 0x0355B6); the literal
pool 0x035462..0x03551A sits in the middle of the body and is jumped over.

CONTRACT
  inputs    byte [0xFFFF7912] (enable), byte [0xFFFF7941] (mode), byte [0xFFFF782B],
            bit4 of byte [0xFFFFA3AE], [0xFFFF78B0] f32, [0xFFFF7920] f32
  output    none in registers
  effects   only when byte [7912] == 1: writes 0xFFFF78C4, 78C8, 78CC, 78D0, 78D4,
            78D8, 78DC, 78E0. When [7912] != 1 it writes NO RAM.

MECHANISM
  if byte [7912] != 1: return
  A = [0xFFFF78B0] ; M = [0xFFFF7920]            ; M is this pass's copy of [63CC]
  if byte [7941] == 0:                            ; set A
      [78C4] = [0xCC0FC] * lookup(0xAC558, A) * lookup(0xAC56C, M)     ; Kp, [CC0FC] = -1.25
      [78C8] = [0xCC0B0]                                               ; Ki = -0.001
      [78CC] = lookup(0xACF3C, A) ; [78D0] = lookup(0xACF50, A)        ; P-term limits
      if byte [782B] == 0: [78D4] = lookup(0xACF64, M) ; [78E0] = lookup(0xACF78, M)
      else:                [78D4] = lookup(0xACFDC, M) ; [78E0] = lookup(0xACFF0, M)
  else:                                           ; set B (any non-zero mode byte)
      [78C4] = [0xCC100] * lookup(0xAC580, A) * lookup(0xAC594, M)     ; [CC100] = 0.0 -> Kp = 0
      [78C8] = [0xCC104]                                               ; Ki = -0.001
      [78CC] = lookup(0xACF8C, A) ; [78D0] = lookup(0xACFA0, A)
      if byte [782B] == 0: [78D4] = lookup(0xACFB4, M) ; [78E0] = lookup(0xACFC8, M)
      else:                [78D4] = lookup(0xACFDC, M) ; [78E0] = lookup(0xACFF0, M)
  [78D8] = [0xCC108] (= 0.07) if bit4 of byte [0xFFFFA3AE] is set, else 0.0
  [78DC] = max([78D4], [78D8])

  Edge cases: the mode test here is "== 0" (0x0353FC tst), while 0x0355B8 and
  0x035218 test the same byte "== 1" -- a mode byte of 2 takes set B here and
  the "not 1" branch there. The r5 != 0 path (0x0353E0..0x0353E4) is dead: r5 is
  loaded with the constant 0 two instructions before it is tested.

EVIDENCE
  enable gate 0x0353EA..0x0353F2; mode split 0x0353F8..0x035402; [782B] splits
  0x03544A/0x03544C and 0x035560/0x035562 (both stores sit in delay slots and run
  on either outcome); bit4 select 0x035588..0x035596; max 0x0355A2.
  r11 = literal 0xFFFF7920: @(r0,r11) with r0 = -112 is 0xFFFF78B0.
  emulated: emu_hole9_afl.py section C, 3,240 cases (en x mode x [782B] x [A3AE]
  x A x M) against independent interpolation of desc_types.read_table(), 2e-6.

==== FUNCTION 0x0355B8 .. 0x03575E  (211 instructions, 1 caller) ====
Caller: the tail bra at 0x034F24. Ends at rts 0x03575A (+ slot 0x03575C). The two
floats this hole is about are the pool words at 0x0357C8 and 0x0357CC, read by
mova at 0x03572E and 0x035732.

CONTRACT
  inputs    [0xFFFF63CC], [0xFFFF318C], byte [0xFFFF7912], byte [0xFFFF7941],
            [78B4] [78B8] [78BC] [78C0] [78C4] [78C8] [78CC] [78D0] [78DC] [78E0]
            state: [78E8] [78FC] [7908] [792C]
            ROM:   0x0CC10C (0.25), 0x0357A8 (eps), 0x0357C8, 0x0357CC, four tables
  output    none in registers (fr14, fr15, r14 are saved and restored)
  effects   always:            [78E4] = E ; [792C] = [78B4]
            byte [7912] != 1:  [78E8] = E ; nothing else (0xFFFF7904 untouched)
            byte [7912] == 1:  [78EC] [78F0] [78F4] [78E8] [78F8] [7900] [7930]
                               [7934] [7908] [78FC] and [0xFFFF7904]

MECHANISM  (every line is one rounding to float32, toward zero; order as executed)
  L     = [0xFFFF318C]
  E     = ([792C] - [78B8]) * [78BC] - ([78B4] - [78B8])            -> [78E4]
  if byte [7912] != 1:  [78E8] = E ; [792C] = [78B4] ; return
  F     = lpf(new = E, old = [78E8], k = [0xCC10C], eps = 1.9073486e-05)   -> [78EC]
  lo1   = lookup(0xAD004 if byte [7941] == 1 else 0xAD02C, [63CC])  -> [78F0]
  hi1   = lookup(0xAD018 if byte [7941] == 1 else 0xAD040, [63CC])  -> [78F4]
  e2    = min(F, hi1) if (0.0 > E) else max(F, lo1)                 -> [78E8]
  P     = clamp(e2 * [78C4], lo = [78CC], hi = [78D0])              -> [78F8]
  S     = [7908] + e2
  I     = [78C8] * S
  total = (P + I) + [78C0]                                          -> [7900]
  limlo = L - [78DC]                                                -> [7930]
  limhi = [78E0] + L                                                -> [7934]
  if (total > limlo) and (limhi > total):  [7908] = S ; [78FC] = I
  else:                                    [7908], [78FC] keep their old values
  inner = clamp(total, lo = limlo, hi = limhi)
  [0xFFFF7904] = clamp(inner, lo = [ROM 0x0357CC], hi = [ROM 0x0357C8])
  [792C] = [78B4]

  Notes that follow from the bytes:
  * With [78BC] = 1 the error is a pure difference of successive samples
    ([792C] - [78B4]); with [78BC] = 0 it is ([78B8] - [78B4]). The calibration
    values the sibling 0x035340 writes are 0.98 / 0.995, i.e. mostly the former.
  * The accumulator is frozen (not reset) whenever the total is on or outside
    the window; the output still follows the clamped total of that pass.
  * All four e2-limit tables hold -0.5 (0xAD004, 0xAD02C) or +0.5 (0xAD018,
    0xAD040) in every cell, in stock and tuned.
  * clamp(x, lo, hi) is 0x0BE56C: "not (x > lo) -> lo; not (hi > x) -> hi; else x".
    With lo = hi = 0.0 the first test returns lo for x <= 0 or NaN and the second
    returns hi for every x > 0: the result is +0.0 (bits 0x00000000) for all x.
  * A NaN in L makes limlo/limhi NaN; the inner clamp then returns NaN and the
    outer clamp returns its lo (stock -0.5, tuned 0.0). Not reachable through
    0x0BDBCC, which refuses to store a NaN.

WHAT THE ZEROING CHANGES, NUMERICALLY
  stock bounds (-0.5, +0.5):  [7904] = clamp(clamp(total, limlo, limhi), -0.5, +0.5)
  tuned bounds ( 0.0,  0.0):  [7904] = +0.0
  Emulated over 1,535 cases (35 hand-picked edge cases + 1,500 random; tuned
  image vs the same image with only 0x0357C8/CC restored):
      enabled   931 cases: tuned result is +0.0 in 931/931; the restored image
                gives the double clamp. It differs from tuned in 930/931 -- that
                count is a property of THIS grid (the tie is the all-zero case),
                not of the routine. Max magnitude 0.5.
      disabled  604 cases: [7904] untouched in both images, 604/604
      the 12 other outputs are bit-identical between the images in 1,535/1,535
  Selected rows (E, total, window edges, then [7904] with stock / tuned bounds):
;   case                          en mode       E       total     limlo    limhi |  stock     tuned
;   base                           1  0    0.0216   -0.006655    -0.01     0.03 | -0.006655   0
;   E < 0                          1  0   -0.0216    0.006855    -0.01     0.03 |  0.006855   0
;   total above the window         1  0    0.0216    0.053345    -0.01     0.03 |  0.03       0

;   L = +0.60 (beyond stock bound) 1  0    0.0216    0.593344     0.58     0.62 |  0.5        0
;   L = -0.60                      1  0    0.0216   -0.606655    -0.62    -0.58 | -0.5        0
;   window 0.46..0.53, total above 1  0    0.0216    0.553344     0.46     0.53 |  0.5        0
;   window 0.46..0.53, total in it 1  0    0.0216    0.463345     0.46     0.53 |  0.463345   0
;   wide window, total +1.99       1  0    0.0216    1.993240    -4.99     5.01 |  0.5        0

;   L = NaN (window edges NaN)     1  0    0.0216   -0.006655      NaN      NaN | -0.5        0
;   window inverted (lo > hi)      1  0    0.0216   -0.006655     0.06    -0.04 |  0.06       0
;   byte [7912] = 0                0  0    0.0216       -           -        -  |  unchanged  unchanged
;   byte [7912] = 2                2  0    0.0216       -           -        -  |  unchanged  unchanged
  WHEN THE STOCK OUTER CLAMP CHANGES THE VALUE (asserted on all 931 enabled cases):
  exactly when inner = clamp(total, limlo, limhi) is NaN (-> -0.5), below -0.5 or
  above +0.5. For a finite inner that needs a window edge beyond +-0.5 AND inner
  (the clamped total, which may be that edge) out there with it -- the raw total
  need not be. The window does not have to lie beyond +-0.5 as a whole: of
  the 100 binding cases in the grid, 44 have a window that straddles zero (for
  example the two "wide window" rows), 39 a window wholly beyond +-0.5 (for
  example the two L = +-0.60 rows; there a total of 0.0 would bind as well),
  16 a window that crosses +-0.5 on one side, and 1 a NaN inner.
  Whether the stock module can get there at all is a separate question, answered
  in PART 5.3 and emu section I: after init it cannot. 0x0359A4 keeps |L| <= 0.06
  and the window is at most 0.07 below and 0.03 above L, so stock [7904] stays
  within -0.13 .. +0.09 (700 random enabled passes: the outer clamp never changed
  the value).

EVIDENCE
  E: 0x0355D6..0x0355F8. Gate: 0x0355FA..0x035604. Filter: 0x035608..0x03561E.
  Table select: 0x035620..0x035650 (cmp/eq #1 at 0x035626). Sign split:
  0x035656..0x03565A, fcmp/gt fr8,fr9 with fr9 = 0.0, fr8 = E -> T = (0.0 > E);
  bt -> min (0x0BE970) with hi1, fall-through -> max (0x0BE960) with lo1.
  P: 0x035680..0x03569A, fr5 = [78CC] is lo, fr6 = [78D0] is hi.
  Total: 0x03569C..0x0356C0. Window: 0x0356C2..0x0356DA.
  Commit/hold: 0x0356E8 fcmp/gt fr8,fr9 (fr9 = total, fr8 = limlo) T = total > limlo;
  0x0356F8 fcmp/gt fr9,fr8 (fr8 = limhi, fr9 = total) T = limhi > total.
  Inner clamp 0x035714..0x035728; outer clamp 0x03572A..0x035736 with
  fr6 <- [0x0357C8] (hi) and fr5 <- [0x0357CC] (lo); store 0x035738..0x03573E.
  emulated: emu_hole9_afl.py sections A (helpers, 582 cases) and B (1,535 cases),
  bit-exact against model_355B8(); section D5 runs 120 consecutive passes of the
  whole 0x034EF4 chain in both images: tuned output +0.0 on every pass, restored
  image peaks at 0.0158 for a 0.60 -> 0.80 -> 0.60 step on [0xFFFF43FC], and all
  internal state stays bit-identical between the two images.

################################################################################
PART 2 -- HELPERS (argument order and NaN behaviour, because everything above
depends on them)
################################################################################

==== FUNCTION 0x0BE56C .. 0x0BE586  (13 instructions, 129 callers) ====
CONTRACT   fr4 = x, fr5 = lo, fr6 = hi  ->  fr0. Uses fr7. No memory access.
MECHANISM  if not (x > lo): return lo
           if not (hi > x): return hi
           return x
           lo is tested first, so lo wins when lo > hi and x <= lo; NaN x -> lo.
EVIDENCE   0x0BE56C fcmp/gt fr5,fr4 -> T = fr4 > fr5; 0x0BE576 fcmp/gt fr4,fr6 ->
           T = fr6 > fr4. emulated: section A, 70 cases incl. NaN, Inf, -0.0,
           lo > hi; lo = hi = 0.0 returns bits 0x00000000 for all 14 x values.
LISTING
0BE56C: F455  fcmp/gt fr5,fr4               ; T = (fr4 > fr5) = (x > lo)
0BE56E: 8D02  bt/s 0x0BE576                 ; bt/s: x > lo -> test hi
0BE570: 0009  nop
0BE572: A006  bra 0x0BE582                  ; x <= lo, or NaN involved: return lo
0BE574: F75C  fmov fr5,fr7                  ; delay slot: fr7 = fr5 = lo
0BE576: F645  fcmp/gt fr4,fr6               ; T = (fr6 > fr4) = (hi > x)
0BE578: 8D02  bt/s 0x0BE580                 ; bt/s: hi > x -> return x
0BE57A: 0009  nop
0BE57C: A001  bra 0x0BE582                  ; x >= hi, or hi NaN: return hi
0BE57E: F76C  fmov fr6,fr7                  ; delay slot: fr7 = fr6 = hi
0BE580: F74C  fmov fr4,fr7                  ; fr7 = x
0BE582: 000B  rts
0BE584: F07C  fmov fr7,fr0                  ; delay slot: fr0 = result

==== FUNCTION 0x0BE960 .. 0x0BE970  (8 instructions, 166 callers) ====
CONTRACT   fr4, fr5 -> fr0 = fr4 if fr4 > fr5 else fr5   (maximum; fr5 on NaN)
EVIDENCE   0x0BE960 fcmp/gt fr5,fr4 -> T = fr4 > fr5. emulated: section A.
LISTING
0BE960: F455  fcmp/gt fr5,fr4               ; T = (fr4 > fr5)
0BE962: 8F02  bf/s 0x0BE96A                 ; bf/s: fr4 not greater -> fr5
0BE964: 0009  nop
0BE966: A001  bra 0x0BE96C
0BE968: F64C  fmov fr4,fr6                  ; delay slot: fr6 = fr4
0BE96A: F65C  fmov fr5,fr6                  ; fr6 = fr5
0BE96C: 000B  rts
0BE96E: F06C  fmov fr6,fr0                  ; delay slot: fr0 = max(fr4, fr5); fr5 on a tie or NaN

==== FUNCTION 0x0BE970 .. 0x0BE980  (8 instructions, 155 callers) ====
CONTRACT   fr4, fr5 -> fr0 = fr4 if fr5 > fr4 else fr5   (minimum; fr5 on NaN)
EVIDENCE   0x0BE970 fcmp/gt fr4,fr5 -> T = fr5 > fr4. emulated: section A.

0BE97C: 000B  rts

==== FUNCTION 0x0BEA40 .. 0x0BEA68  (20 instructions, 101 callers) ====
CONTRACT   fr4 = new, fr5 = old, fr6 = k, fr7 = eps -> fr0. Clobbers r0, r4, fpul.
MECHANISM  if old is Inf or NaN: return new
           r = new + (1 - k) * (old - new)        ; = k*new + (1-k)*old, one fmac
           return new if eps > |new - r| else r
EVIDENCE   exponent test 0x0BEA40..0x0BEA4A; fmac 0x0BEA54; snap 0x0BEA5C

Three writers of 0xFFFF7904 were found, all through table slot +76:
    0x034F72 (init)   0x035046 (reload)   0x03573E (the clamped controller output)
and no other by any search available (a store through a base built by arithmetic
cannot be excluded by scanning). The literal 0xFFFF7904 occurs in 8 places in the ROM --
7 literal pools whose single loader is each followed by a READ (listed in PART 4)
and the table slot 0x063AD4. The table base is loaded at 7 sites (above); offset
76 is used at 0x034F6E/0x034F74, 0x035042 and 0x035738 only.
scripts/mapping/find_writers.py FFFF7904 --reads (direct, displacement, indexed
and GBR-relative forms) reports 0 writes -- it cannot see a store through a
pointer fetched from a ROM table -- and the same 7 reads plus a phantom
(0x035A0E is the low half of the pool literal 0xFFFF6A22 at 0x035A0C).
The same tool on 0xFFFF7910..0xFFFF7913 (count, enable byte, hysteresis byte)
reports one "write", 0x0351BE, which is the low half of the pool literal
0x000CC094 at 0x0351BC: the enable byte's only stores found are 0x035106 and
0x0351DE. It also lists nine literal-pool READERS of the enable byte 0xFFFF7912
outside this module (0x026856, 0x033AEA, 0x0358C0, 0x035DEE, 0x066726, 0x07352C,
0x074620, 0x080568, 0x080EA6); only 0x0358C0, 0x035DEE and 0x066726 were looked at.

                    not (lookup > [63D4])       (2-D lookup 0xAD944; T must be 0)
                    byte [7453] == 1            byte [7BE2] == 0
            otherwise byte [0xFFFF7912] = 0 and word [7910] = 0
  For ordinary numbers "not (c > x)" reads as x >= c. For a NaN it does not:
  fcmp/gt is false whenever an operand is NaN, so
      a NaN in [6624] or in d             DISABLES      (the two tests that need T = 1)
      a NaN in [63F8], [6350] or [63D4]   does NOT disable (the three "not" tests)
  The byte tests are exact: [7448], [63A4], [7453] must be exactly 1 (2, 0x81 fail);
  [94B1], [9711], [7BE2] must be exactly 0.

---- LIFECYCLE OF 0xFFFF7904 AND 0xFFFF318C IN THE TUNED IMAGE (emulated, section H) ----
Start: a value 0.02 left in the record 0xFFFF318C (valid checksum). Then init
(0x0357D0, 0x034EC8 -- the order in 0x047C40), three periodic passes of
0x034EF4 followed by 0x03580C (the order in the dispatcher 0x049CF0), init again.
(State that init does not write -- emu section D1b lists it -- is zeroed
explicitly for this run, as after a RAM clear; nothing else is pre-set.)
;   tuned image, loop enabled           [318C]    [790C]    [7904]     [7944]
;     after init 1                      +0.0200   +0.0200   +0.030000  +0.030000
;     after pass 1                       0.0      +0.0200    0.0       +0.029941
;     after pass 3                       0.0      +0.0200    0.0       +0.029825
;     after init 2                       0.0       0.0       0.0        0.0
;   tuned image, loop never enabled (byte [7448] = 0)
;     after init 1                      +0.0200   +0.0200   +0.030000  +0.030000
;     after pass 1..3                    0.0      +0.0200   +0.030000  +0.030000
;     after init 2                       0.0       0.0       0.0        0.0
;   both pairs restored (= stock module), loop enabled
;     after pass 1                      +0.0200   +0.0200   +0.029529  +0.029999
;     after pass 3                      +0.0200   +0.0200   +0.029050  +0.029996
;     after init 2                      +0.0200   +0.0200   +0.030000  +0.030000
What this establishes for the tuned image:
  * 0x0359A4 runs on each of these calls of 0x03580C (asserted per call, with the
    emulator's executed-set cleared first) and its exit store forces [0xFFFF318C]
    to 0.0 on the first pass. It does NOT run on a call entered with byte
    [0xFFFF7986] == 0 (PART 6, 0x03580C). Emulated: the same periodic pair with
    no init and byte [7986] = 0 -- pass 1 skips 0x0359A4 and [318C] stays 0.02,
    pass 2 runs it and stores 0.0.
  * With the loop enabled [0xFFFF7904] is 0.0 from the first pass.
  * With the loop never enabled, the init value of [0xFFFF7904] (0.03 here) stays
    until the next init, even though [318C] is already 0.0.
  * After the next init every quantity is 0.0 and stays 0.0: all three writers
    of [0xFFFF7904] then produce 0.0.
So unless something re-plants a non-zero 0xFFFF318C (only the unclamped init
rewrite in 0x035E88 could, and only from a value already outside -0.024..0.028),
the steady state of the car is [0xFFFF7904] = 0.0 and [0xFFFF318C] = 0.0.

EVIDENCE  bytes; emulated: section E1 -- [77BC] against the formula for 12 values
          of [7904] x 2 values of [7BDC], plus three runs with every other term
          non-zero; slope d[77BC]/d[7904] = 1.000000 inside the clamps.

0xFFFF77C8 has 18 direct readers (find_writers --reads: 0x021B6C, 0x02451C,
0x0316AA, 0x032250, 0x03319A, 0x0342D8, 0x0348AA, 0x034FF8, 0x039F60, 0x067080,
0x067A2E, 0x067D88, 0x069872, 0x071B0E, 0x073502, 0x074618, 0x083F96, 0x0A92C4).
One of them is the enable gate above (0x034FF8: [77C8] - [318C] must be in
0.895 .. 1.036), so the loop's own output feeds back into its own enable.
0xFFFF77C8 has four stores (find_writers; each decoded): 0x033596, 0x033610 and
0x033634 in 0x033460, and 0x0332C6 in 0x0332A2, which is NOT part of 0x033460 and
writes 1.0 to 77BC, 77C0, 77C4 and 77C8 (section below). So 0x033460 is not the
only source of 0xFFFF77C8.
What 0xFFFF77C8 physically is was not traced here.

---- 5.3  the 0x03580C chain: filter into 0xFFFF7944, vote, step and store 0xFFFF318C ----
0x03580C (listed in PART 6) calls, in this order, 0x0358A0, 0x0359A4, 0x035DE4 and
0x035E2C, and tail-jumps to 0x035E64. All five are listed here in full. They
share a SECOND pointer table at 0x063B00:
;   +0  -> 0xFFFF318C   L, the stored centre of the [7904] window
;   +4  -> 0xFFFF7944   filtered copy of [7904]
;   +8  -> 0xFFFF794A   u16 calls since the last reset
;   +12 -> 0xFFFF794C   u16 vote counter (reset value 0x0200)
;   +16 -> 0xFFFF7988   u16 consecutive calls with byte [7950] == 1
;   +20 -> 0xFFFF7950   u8 "conditions met" byte
;   +24 -> 0xFFFF794E   u8 count        +28 -> 0xFFFF794F   u8 count
The base registers 0xFFFF7988 (r10 in 0x0359A4) and 0xFFFF7984 (r5 in 0x0358A0)
are literals; their @(r0,Rn) forms are resolved in the comments. The values at
0xFFFF7954..0xFFFF7970 are plain copies latched by 0x03580C (PART 6).

==== FUNCTION 0x0359A4 .. 0x035DE4  (382 instructions, 1 caller) ====
Caller: bsr at 0x03587C in 0x03580C. The function has no rts: its single exit is
the tail bra at 0x035DE0 into 0x035F48, reached from every path.

CONTRACT
  inputs    [0xFFFF7904], [0xFFFF318C], [0xFFFF7944]; bytes [7950] [7913] [A824] [A825]
            [798A]; words [7988] [794A] [794C]; bytes [794E] [794F]; latched copies
            [7958] ([63CC]), [7964] ([6624]), [796C] ([63F8]), [7970] ([63C4])
  output    none in registers
  effects   always:  [7974] = lookup(0xAD068, [7970]) ; [7978] = lookup(0xAD07C, [7970])
                     [0xFFFF318C] = clamp(L', lo = [ROM 0x036000], hi = [ROM 0x035FFC])
                     through 0x035F48 (record store with checksum words)
            byte [7950] == 1:  [0xFFFF7944] = lpf(new = [7904], old = [7944], k,
                               eps = 1.5258789e-05), k = [7974] if byte [7913] == 1
                               else [7978] (both tables hold 0.00195312 everywhere)
            then either the RESET BLOCK or one VOTE and at most one STEP of L

MECHANISM
  L = [318C] ; x = [7944] after the filter
  RESET (0x035DB4) when ANY of:  byte [7950] != 1 ; word [7988] < 31 ([0xCBC4C]) ;
        8.0 > [7958] ([0xCC130]) ; byte [798A] != 0 ; word [794A] >= 156 ([0xCBC56])
      word [794A] = 0 ; word [794C] = 0x0200 ; byte [794E] = 0 ; byte [794F] = 0 ; L' = L
  otherwise:
      word [794A] += 1                       ; calls in this period, 1 .. 156
      D = x - L
      FIRST SET  (byte [A824] != 1 and byte [A825] != 1):
          a = 2-D lookup(0xADA5C, [796C], [7964])  -> [797C]   (-0.01 in every cell)
          b = 2-D lookup(0xADA70, [796C], [7964])  -> [7980]   (+0.013 in every cell)
          a2 = a + [0xCC148] (0.0) ; b2 = b + [0xCC14C] (0.0)
          vote:  not (b > D)      -> word [794C] += 1 (saturating)
                 else if a > D    -> word [794C] -= 1 (not below 0)
          not (b2 > x) -> byte [794E] += 1 ;  else if a2 > x -> byte [794F] += 1
          step:  word [794C] >= 668 ([0xCBC54])       L' = min(L + 0.0005, +0.05)       site 1
                 else word [794C] < 357 ([0xCBC52])   L' = max(L - 0.0005, -0.05)       site 2
                 else if word [794A] == 156:
                     not (0 > L) and byte [794E] < 1 ([0xCBBF0])
                                                      L' = clamp(L - 0.0005, 0, +0.05)  site 3
                     (0 > L) and byte [794F] < 1 ([0xCBBF1])
                                                      L' = clamp(L + 0.0005, -0.05, 0)  site 4
      SECOND SET (byte [A824] == 1 or byte [A825] == 1):
          vote:  not (0.0 > D) ([0xCC138]) -> word [794C] += 1
                 else if 0.0 > D ([0xCC134]) -> word [794C] -= 1
          step:  word [794C] >= 668 ([0xCBC50])       L' = min(L + 0.0005, +0.06)       site 5
                 else word [794C] < 357 ([0xCBC4E])   L' = max(L - 0.0005, -0.06)       site 6
  [0xFFFF318C] = clamp(L', [0x036000], [0x035FFC])    ; stock -0.5 .. +0.5, tuned 0 .. 0

  What follows from the bytes (each line emulated, section E3):
  * The vote counter restarts at 512 and moves by one per call, and a period is
    156 voting calls followed by one reset call. 512 + 156 = 668 and
    512 - 156 = 356, so a step up or down needs 156 votes the same way and can
    only happen on the 156th call: AT MOST ONE 0.0005 STEP PER 157 CALLS.
    (D held at +0.02: L steps on calls 156 and 313, site 1; at -0.02: site 2;
    with byte [A824] = 1: sites 5 and 6.)
  * With the count left between 357 and 667, the 156th call lets L decay by
    0.0005 toward zero (sites 3 and 4) unless the filtered output was at or
    above b2 (site 3) or below a2 (site 4) at least once in the period. Those
    two tests use [7944] itself, not [7944] - L.
  * Stock module: |L| <= 0.06 on entry gives |L| <= 0.06 on exit. From
    |L| <= 0.05 one call moves L by at most 0.0005. An L between 0.05 and 0.06
    (reachable through sites 5 and 6) can be pulled back by up to 0.01 in ONE
    call by a +-0.05 site. An |L| above 0.06 never grows.
  * Tuned ROM: every exit stores +0.0 (2,511 states).
  * A NaN L: site 3 turns it into 0.0; with no step the stock store writes
    -0.5 (0x0BE56C returns lo for a NaN) and the tuned store 0.0.

EVIDENCE
  filter 0x035ABE..0x035AE8; reset tests 0x035AEE, 0x035AFE (cmp/hs r2,r6: T = word
  [7988] >= 31), 0x035B0E (fcmp/gt fr8,fr9: T = 8.0 > [7958], bf goes on), 0x035B18,
  0x035B28; votes 0x035BA6 / 0x035BC0 and 0x035CBC / 0x035CD6; step selects 0x035C1A,
  0x035C3A, 0x035C5A, 0x035C64, 0x035C70, 0x035C8A, 0x035C9A, 0x035CF6, 0x035D9C;
  sites 0x035C2A, 0x035C4A, 0x035C82, 0x035CB0, 0x035D06, 0x035DAC; reset block
  0x035DB4..0x035DCA; exit 0x035DCC..0x035DE2. After 0x035ABC, fr15 is written only
  in the delay slot that follows each of the six sites.
  emulated: emu_hole9_afl.py section E3 -- model_359A4() bit-exact on 5,022 states
  (stock module and tuned image; 5,000 random, 22 directed decay cases incl. -0.0
  and NaN). Each state is also checked for WHICH site ran and for leaving
  through 0x035DE0 -> 0x035F48. Five 314-call period runs.
;   step-site hits in that run:   1: 139   2: 157   3: 14   4: 6   5: 101   6: 109   none: 4,496

therefore not necessarily taken on the same pass; the rates are not established.

WHO ELSE READS THE COPY 0xFFFF9340. The literal 0xFFFF9340 occurs in the ROM once,
as table slot 0x097524, and the only store found is 0x06673E. The table base
0x00097520 has seven PC-relative loaders: 0x066770, 0x0668B6, 0x066906 (the three
functions above) and 0x066D50, 0x066E1C, 0x066EBC, 0x066F94. The last four hand it
to code (0x066D5E, 0x066E2E, 0x066EBC, 0x066F94) that uses offsets +40 .. +60 only;
no "mov.l @(4,Rtable)" occurs there (checked mechanically in emu section E2e).
So the copy has exactly four reads: 0x0667D2, 0x0667F0, 0x0668D4, 0x066924.

---- 5.5  scaled integer exports ---------------------------------------------
0x0BE5A8(fr4 = x, fr5 = scale, fr6 = offset) -> r0 = min(max(trunc((x-offset)/scale + 0.5), 0), 255)
0x0BE5D8 is the same with 65535. Both decoded from bytes and exercised below.

047F5A: 0009  nop
;   pool 0x0480D0: .long 0x000357D0  ROM
;   pool 0x0480D4: .long 0x00034EC8  ROM

################################################################################
PART 7 -- WHERE THE REGULATED INPUT 0xFFFF43FC COMES FROM
################################################################################
==== FUNCTION 0x00C5FC .. 0x00C612  (11 instructions, 2 callers) ====
Callers: 0x00DF14, 0x00E3B2.
CONTRACT   [0xFFFF43FC] = float(u16 [0xFFFF4050]) * 7.6293945e-05
           The constant is 0x38A00000 = 5/65536 exactly, and the product is exact
           in float32, so [43FC] is the 16-bit word scaled to 0 .. 4.99992.
           This is the only store to 0xFFFF43FC found. find_writers also lists
           0x00C482 "mov.l r0,@(16,r11)": that address is the low half of the
           pool literal 0x00011B04 at 0x00C480 (bytes 00 01 1B 04), not code.
EVIDENCE   bytes; emulated: section J, seven words incl. 0x0000 and 0xFFFF, and
           the only RAM written is [43FC].
LISTING
00C5FC: D414  mov.l @(0x00C650),r4          ; = 0xFFFF4050 RAM
00C5FE: 6441  mov.w @r4,r4                  ; r4 = word [0xFFFF4050]
00C600: D314  mov.l @(0x00C654),r3          ; = 0xFFFF43FC RAM
00C602: 644D  extu.w r4,r4                  ; r4 = zero-extended
00C604: C714  mova @(0x00C658),r0           ; -> 0x00C658: long 0x38A00000 f32 7.629395e-05
00C606: 445A  lds r4,fpul                   ; fpul = r4
00C608: F208  fmov.s @r0,fr2                ; fr2 = 7.6293945e-05 = 5/65536 exactly
00C60A: F32D  float fpul,fr3                ; fr3 = float(u16)
00C60C: F322  fmul fr2,fr3                  ; fr3 = u16 * 5/65536
00C60E: 000B  rts
00C610: F33A  fmov.s fr3,@r3                ; delay slot: [0xFFFF43FC] = fr3     <-- the only store to 0xFFFF43FC found
;   pool 0x00C650: .long 0xFFFF4050  RAM
;   pool 0x00C654: .long 0xFFFF43FC  RAM

One store to the word 0xFFFF4050 was found (bytes only; a store through a
computed index cannot be excluded by scanning). It is in 0x0040D4, whose caller
is 0x00DF14, and copies the 16-bit on-chip peripheral register at 0xFFFFF834:
r12 holds 0xFFFFF818 (0x0040F4) and GBR is 0xFFFF4024 (0x0040E4).
LISTING (0x0040F4 and 0x0041D6..0x0041D8 of 0x0040D4)
0040F4: 9CA9  mov.w @(0x00424A),r12         ; = 0xF818 (-2024) -> 0xFFFFF818 PERIPH  | r12 = 0xFFFFF818: an on-chip peripheral register address, not RAM
0041D6: 85CE  mov.w @(28,r12),r0            ; r0 = word at peripheral 0xFFFFF818+28 = 0xFFFFF834
0041D8: C116  mov.w r0,@(44,gbr)            ; gbr+44 = 0xFFFF4050  | word [0xFFFF4050] = that peripheral word
What the register at 0xFFFFF834 is (and, if it is an A/D result register, which
channel) has to come from the SH7058 hardware manual; it is not in the ROM. Which
sensor is wired to that channel is not in the ROM either. (r12 is set once, at
0x0040F4, and restored only at the exit 0x004208; the two helpers the function
calls in between, 0x0BE81C and 0x0BE82C, write only r0, r5, SR and the long
at @r4.)

==== FUNCTION 0x0A8916 .. 0x0A892E  (12 instructions, 1 caller) ====
Caller: 0x0A67E2 (the same caller as the [7904] export 0x0A892E in PART 5.5).
CONTRACT   byte [0xFFFFAF82] = [0xFFFF43FC] / 0.005, rounded, saturated 0..255.
           One count = 0.005 of [43FC]. (0x05D402 divides [43FC] by the same 0.005
           through 0x0BE5D8; read from fn_dump, not listed.)
EVIDENCE   bytes only.
LISTING
0A8916: 4F22  sts.l pr,@-r15
0A8918: D280  mov.l @(0x0A8B1C),r2          ; = 0xFFFF43FC RAM
0A891A: F428  fmov.s @r2,fr4                ; fr4 = [0xFFFF43FC]
0A891C: D27A  mov.l @(0x0A8B08),r2          ; = 0x000BE5A8 ROM
0A891E: F68D  fldi0 fr6                     ; fr6 = offset = 0.0
0A8920: C77F  mova @(0x0A8B20),r0           ; -> 0x0A8B20: long 0x3BA3D70A f32 0.005
0A8922: 420B  jsr @r2                       ; -> 0x0BE5A8  | r0 = sat8(trunc((fr4 - 0)/fr5 + 0.5))
0A8924: F508  fmov.s @r0,fr5                ; delay slot: fr5 = scale = 0.005
0A8926: D27F  mov.l @(0x0A8B24),r2          ; = 0xFFFFAF82 RAM
0A8928: 4F26  lds.l @r15+,pr
0A892A: 000B  rts
0A892C: 2200  mov.b r0,@r2                  ; delay slot: byte [0xFFFFAF82] = [43FC] / 0.005, saturated 0..255
;   pool 0x0A8B08: .long 0x000BE5A8  ROM
;   pool 0x0A8B1C: .long 0xFFFF43FC  RAM
;   pool 0x0A8B24: .long 0xFFFFAF82  RAM

################################################################################
ESTABLISHED
################################################################################
 E1  Table 0x063A88 is 30 pointers into RAM; +76 -> 0xFFFF7904. A second table at
     0x063B00 (318C, 7944, 794A, 794C, 7988, 7950, 794E, 794F) serves the
     0x03580C chain. (bytes; emu sec. 0)
 E2  0x034EF4 writes [7920] = [63CC] and byte [7941] = byte [984D], calls eight
     helpers in a fixed order and tail-jumps to 0x0355B8. (0x034EF4..0x034F26; D1)
 E3  0x0353BC writes gains/limits 78C4..78E0 only when byte [7912] == 1; table
     set chosen by byte [7941] == 0, window tables by byte [782B] == 0, and
     [78DC] = max(table, 0.07 if bit4 of [A3AE] else 0). (listing; emu C)
 E4  0x0355B8 computes E, filters and one-side-limits it, forms
     total = clamp(e2*Kp) + Ki*([7908]+e2) + [78C0], clamps it to
     [318C]-[78DC] .. [318C]+[78E0], then to ROM 0x0357CC .. 0x0357C8, and stores
     that to 0xFFFF7904 -- only when byte [7912] == 1. (listing; emu B)
 E5  0x0BE56C(x, lo = 0.0, hi = 0.0) = +0.0 for every x. Hence tuned
     [0xFFFF7904] = +0.0 on every enabled pass. (0x0BE56C..0x0BE584; emu A, B)
 E6  The zeroing changes no other output of 0x0355B8: accumulator, filter state
     and window edges are bit-identical with either bound pair. (emu B, D5)
 E7  With the stock bounds the stored value is clamp(inner, -0.5, +0.5) with
     inner = clamp(total, limlo, limhi). The outer clamp returns something other
     than inner exactly when inner is NaN (-> -0.5), below -0.5 or above +0.5.
     (0x035714..0x03573E; emu B, asserted on 931 enabled cases)
 E8  0xFFFF7904 has three writers: 0x034F72 (init), 0x035046 (reload on
     byte [6A28] == 1), 0x03573E. Only the last goes through 0x0357C8/CC.
     (table offset 76 is loaded at 0x034F6E, 0x035042, 0x035738 and nowhere else
     in 0x034EC8..0x036007)
 E9  0x035F48 clamps with ROM 0x036000 .. 0x035FFC (also zeroed in every tune)
     and is the store to 0xFFFF318C used by 0x034F94 and by the single exit of
     0x0359A4; tuned stores are always +0.0. (0x035F48..0x035F86; emu F, E3, D3)
     The other stores to 0xFFFF318C: 0x0357E8 writes 0.0; 0x035E88 (init) rewrites
     it to 0.038 when it is not below 0.028 and to -0.028 when it is below
     -0.024, without the clamp. (PART 6; emu G)
 E10 0xFFFF790C occurs in the ROM only as table slot 0x063ADC (+84); offset 84 is
     loaded at 0x034F86 (the store 0x034F8A, with [318C]) and 0x035034 (the read
     0x035038). "find_writers FFFF790C" prints "no writes found" (it cannot see
     a store through a table pointer); run on the range FFFF790C..FFFF790F it
     reports one phantom, for 0xFFFF790E at 0x034E56, which is the low half of
     the pool literal 0x000CC090 at 0x034E54.
 E11 The enable byte 0xFFFF7912 is 1 iff byte [7448] == 1, byte [94B1] == 0,
     6000 > [6624], not (0.895 > d), 1.036 > d, not (0.15 > [63F8]),
     not (-40 > [6350]), byte [9711] == 0, byte [63A4] == 1, not (lookup > [63D4]),
     byte [7453] == 1 and byte [7BE2] == 0, with d = [77C8] - [318C] taken after
     the reload block. A NaN in [6624] or d disables; a NaN in [63F8], [6350] or
     [63D4] does not. Word [7910] counts enabled passes and saturates at 0xFFFF.
     (0x035072..0x03511C, 0x0351D8..0x0351E4; emu D2, D2b)
 E12 0x03580C runs its body, and with it 0x0359A4, iff byte [7986] >= byte
     [0xCBBEF] (= 1) on entry; with this calibration only an entry value of 0
     skips, and every call leaves the byte at 1. (0x035816..0x03581E,
     0x035888..0x035896; emu G4, H)
 E13 0x0359A4 has a single exit, the tail bra 0x035DE0 -> 0x035F48 with r4 = 0 and
     fr4 = L. It filters [7904] into [7944], casts one vote per call and can
     step L by 0.0005 at six sites limited at +-0.05 (sites 1-4) or +-0.06
     (sites 5, 6); a step needs a 156-call period. (PART 5.3 listing; emu E3)
 E14 Stock module: 0x035E88 maps every stored L (NaN and +-Inf included) into
     [-0.028, +0.038]; 0x0359A4 keeps |L| <= 0.06; the window is at most 0.07
     below and 0.03 above L. Hence, once init has run, [7904] stays within
     -0.13 .. +0.09 and the +-0.5 clamp never changes it. (emu G1, E3, I)
 E15 Consumers: 0x0334C6 adds [7904] with weight +1 into the clamped sum stored
     at 0xFFFF77BC (emu E1); 0x032374 is dead while byte [0xCBBDF] != 1 (bytes);
     0x0359C6 filters it into 0xFFFF7944 (emu E3), which is read again at 0x035DEA
     and 0x035E30; 0x06673A copies it to 0xFFFF9340, which has exactly four reads:
     0x0667D2 and 0x0667F0 (0x06665E -> byte 932C), 0x0668D4 (0x0668B4 -> byte 932D),
     0x066924 (0x066904 -> byte 932E) (emu E2c, E2d, E2e); 0x02453A, 0x05DCFE,
     0x0A8932 are scaled exports (emu E2, E2b).
 E16 0x06665E writes ten RAM locations on every call: floats 933C, 9340, 9344,
     9348, 934C, 9350 and bytes 9354, 9355, 9356, 932C. (listing; emu E2c)
 E17 0xFFFF77BC, 77C0, 77C4 and 77C8 are also written by 0x0332A2 (1.0 each, when
     byte [65F6] == 1). 0xFFFF77C8 has four stores: 0x0332C6, 0x033596, 0x033610,
     0x033634. (0x0332C0..0x0332D2; emu E1b)
 E18 [0xFFFF43FC] = u16 [0xFFFF4050] * 5/65536 (0x00C5FC..0x00C610; emu J). The one
     store to the word 0xFFFF4050 that was found copies the peripheral register
     0xFFFFF834 (0x0041D6..0x0041D8; bytes).
 E19 Stock vs 20.19c differ in 0x034EC8..0x036007 in exactly four bytes:
     0x0357C8, 0x0357CC, 0x035FFC, 0x036000. Twenty ROM ranges of calibration and
     pointer tables are identical in stock, 20.19c and all 19 flashed bins, and
     the four longs are zero in all 19. (emu sec. 0)
 E20 Init (0x0357D0 then 0x034EC8) does not write [78E8], [78FC], word [7910],
     bytes [7913], [7940], word [7948], bytes [7951], [7952], word [7988]; each
     is read before it is written on some path of the first periodic pass.
     (emu D1b, run with no RAM pre-set)

################################################################################
NOT ESTABLISHED  (and what would settle each)
################################################################################
 N1  Which sensor 0xFFFF43FC is. Its source is settled as far as the ROM goes
     (E18): a 16-bit word scaled by 5/65536, copied from peripheral 0xFFFFF834.
     Settle the rest with the SH7058 hardware manual (what that register is --
     if it is an A/D result register, which channel) and the ECU pinout.
     Whether 0x0041D8 is the ONLY store to
     0xFFFF4050 is not established either (only fixed-address stores were
     scanned; 0x0040D4 is called from 0x00DF14, and no periodic writer was found).
 N2  What 0xFFFF63CC is. It is the axis of every [63CC]-indexed table here
     (breakpoints 0..75) and is summed into 78B0 (clamped at 76800). Settle by
     tracing its writer, or by finding a DEFINED table that shares one of these
     axis pointers (none of the 28 descriptors used here has a definition).
 N3  What 0xFFFF77BC / 0xFFFF77C8 are, and therefore whether a positive
     0xFFFF7904 means more or less fuel -- or whether it is a fuel term at all
     (see A3 in the comparison section). The bytes give "added to 1.0, clamped
     0.75..1.25"; settle by decoding one of the 18 readers of 0xFFFF77C8 down to
     an injector quantity.
 N4  The rate of 0x034EF4 (how often the dispatcher 0x049CF0 runs), and of
     0x0664E2 (caller 0x04A420), which runs the window tests.
 N5  When 0x047C40 (init) runs (every key-on? only after a reset?), and whether
     RAM is cleared before it. E20 lists the state init leaves alone; the
     lifecycle table in PART 3 and the "skipped call" of 0x03580C (E12) both
     depend on what that state holds at power-up. Whether byte [0xFFFF6A28] (the
     reload trigger) is ever 1 in normal running was not traced either.
 N6  What the inputs of the 0x0359A4 vote are physically: bytes [0xFFFFA824] /
     [0xFFFFA825] (they select the +-0.06 set), byte [0xFFFF798A] = byte [6254],
     and the latched floats. The arithmetic is traced (PART 5.3); the meaning of
     the selectors is not.
 N7  The meaning of the result bytes 0xFFFF932C / 932D / 932E, of 0xFFFFAF83 /
     0xFFFFAFAA, and of the 0xFFFF683C block. Bytes [932D] / [932E] are handed to
     0x066A0C / 0x066ABC together with the constants 0xAD / 0xAE; decoding those
     two (and what the constants index) would say what the tests report.
 N8  Other direct readers that were only glanced at: of 0xFFFF318C -- 0x03A2F4
     (compared with [0xCC5F8]), 0x07DCE2 (compared with [0xC5090] / [0xC5094]),
     0x08C400 (copied to 0xFFFFA828); of the enable byte 0xFFFF7912 -- 0x026856,
     0x033AEA, 0x07352C, 0x074620, 0x080568, 0x080EA6; of the 0x03580C state --
     0x02690C (word [7948]), 0x08C408 (byte [7950]), 0x02770A (byte [7952]).
     With 0xFFFF318C pinned at 0.0 the first three have fixed outcomes; what any
     of them gates was not traced.
 N9  Whether SH-2E silicon rounds FMAC once or twice (affects 0x0BEA40 and the
     table lookups in the last bit only; the tuned result +0.0 is unaffected).
     The rounding MODE is no longer open: toward zero (corrections item 106),
     and the models here use it. Only fused-vs-unfused remains.
 N10 Why the tuner zeroed the 0x0357C8 pair in addition to the 0x035FFC pair.
     The bytes show that either pair alone leaves a path: 0x035FFC alone keeps
     the controller output alive inside the window around 0; 0x0357C8 alone
     keeps the learned centre and the init/reload copies alive.
 N11 Whether a store to 0xFFFF318C, 0xFFFF7904 or 0xFFFF7944 exists that goes
     through a pointer built by arithmetic (for example a generic routine that
     restores checksummed records). Scanning cannot exclude it; the stock bound
     in E14 assumes there is none.
 N12 The branch of 0x033460 that does NOT copy 0xFFFF77BC into 0xFFFF77C8
     (0x0335AA: [77C8] = clamp([7810] + [7818] + 1.0, ..)) and the conditions at
     0x033528..0x033616 that select it were read but not emulated; when that
     branch is taken 0xFFFF7904 does not reach 0xFFFF77C8 at all.

################################################################################
COMPARISON WITH EXISTING CLAIMS
################################################################################
Done last. Sources read: fn_dump --labels on the three entries; grep of docs/,
disassembly/analysis/, disassembly/maps/, disassembly/ghidra/ImportAE5L600L.java
for every address form; docs/verification-status.json; the definition XMLs via
scripts/defs.py; .pipeline/items/afc-clamp-zeroed/settle.md.
Disclosure: the "label_claim" strings in function_census.json were seen while
pulling caller counts for the section headings, and scripts/defs.py was queried
for the scalar addresses, both AFTER PARTS 1-5 and the emulation were fixed.
No reading in PARTS 1-5 was changed by anything found here. PART 6 was added
because of it (to test names, not to adopt them).

A. CLAIMS THE BYTES CONTRADICT OR DO NOT SUPPORT
------------------------------------------------
 A1  docs/open-holes.md:365-366  "With both bounds 0, the value stored to
     0xFFFF7904 is always 0.0."
     True of the store at 0x03573E only. That store is skipped when byte
     [0xFFFF7912] != 1 (0x035604), and 0x034F72 / 0x035046 write 0xFFFF7904 without the
     clamp. Emulated (D3): tuned image, reload with the loop off -> [7904] =
     [790C] = 0.0125. MISLEADING.
 A2  docs/open-holes.md:364-365  "the pointer struct at ROM 0x063A88 (all
     0xFFFF78B4-0xFFFF7941, plus 0xFFFF318C)".
     The table this routine uses is 30 pointers (+0..+116). The long at +120
     (0xFFFF318C) is entry 0 of a second table whose base 0x00063B00 has its own
     pool slots (0x035A2C, 0x035FB8); 0x0355B8 reads 0xFFFF318C through its own
     literal 0x0357A0 (0x0355D2). The same slip is in
     .pipeline/items/afc-clamp-zeroed/settle.md ("Struct longs (31)"). IMPRECISE.
 A3  docs/open-holes.md:347 (title, "an A/F-correction term") and
     docs/allocation.json:26 (id "afc_clamp_zeroed").
     UNSUPPORTED AS AN IDENTITY, NOT CONTRADICTED. Nothing in the bytes names
     the quantity, and nothing in them conflicts with the title (see REVIEW
     HISTORY). What they show: the output of the 0x0355B8 controller, added with
     weight +1 into the "1.0 + ..." sum stored at 0xFFFF77BC. Consistent with
     the title, each point read from bytes here:
       * the regulated input is a 16-bit word copied from the on-chip
         peripheral register 0xFFFFF834 and scaled by 5/65536 (PART 7), held
         toward a setpoint of 0.70 or 0.6787 with this calibration (0x035218)
         and exported at 0.005 per count (0x0A8916);
       * the output is exported as (100*x + 100)/0.78125, a percentage centred
         on 128 (0x0A892E), and is added into a factor clamped 0.75 .. 1.25;
       * the twin pair 0x035FFC/0x036000 in the same module carries the
         definition name "AF 3 Correction Limits".
     What keeps it unproven: that definition exists ONLY in the project XML
     (scripts/defs.py reports source 'project', base_matched False -- there is
     no table of that name in 32BITBASE.xml), none of the 28 descriptors the
     module uses has a definition, and no reader of 0xFFFF77C8 was followed down
     to an injector quantity.
 A4  docs/open-holes.md:368-369  "unrecorded in any definition or tune note".
     Correct for 0x0357C8/CC, but the twin pair 0x035FFC/0x036000 -- same module,
     same stock values, zeroed in the same revs -- IS defined: project XML line
     72, "AF 3 Correction Limits" (a project-only entry: no table of that name
     in 32BITBASE.xml), whose description says zeroing it is a deliberate
     disable. Hole 9 does not mention the twin; the two pairs are two halves of
     one edit. IMPRECISE (incomplete).
 A5  definitions/AE5L600L 2013 USDM Impreza WRX MT.xml:72 (description of
     "AF 3 Correction Limits") and ImportAE5L600L.java:1519-1520 ("Setting to 0
     disables rear O2 input on target AFR").
     By bytes this pair bounds only the value STORED to 0xFFFF318C (0x035F60..
     0x035F76). With it zeroed and 0x0357C8/CC left at stock the controller
     still writes a non-zero 0xFFFF7904 inside the window around 0 (emulated D2:
     +0.010139 on an enabled pass from a cold state). The disable is complete only with
     both pairs. "Rear O2" and "target AFR" are not established by the bytes
     either way. The '+' / '-' cell labels (0x35FFC / 0x36000) do match the bytes
     (hi / lo). IMPRECISE.
 A6  disassembly/maps/disassembly.txt:3301-3302  "0x35FFC ... Limits Min",
     "0x36000 ... Limits Max"; :1279 "00036000: AF 3 Learning Limits"; :2882
     "The rear O2 sensor has NO influence on fuel trim".
     Min/max are swapped: 0x035FFC is loaded into fr6 = hi (0x035F60/0x035F62,
     stock +0.5), 0x036000 into fr5 = lo (0x035F64/0x035F68, stock -0.5). 0x036000
     is the second cell of the same clamp, not another table. The "no
     influence" conclusion does not follow from this pair alone (A5). WRONG.
     (That file is already declared non-authoritative.)
 A7  ImportAE5L600L.java:5371 "evap_pressure_baseline" (0xFFFF318C);
     disassembly/maps/ram_reference.txt:637; disassembly/analysis/
     diag_tasks_analysis.txt:259, :298, :614 ("Pressure baseline").
     0xFFFF318C is the checksum-protected centre of the 0xFFFF7904 window
     (0x0356C2..0x0356DA), copied into 0xFFFF7904 at init and reload, stepped by
     0.0005 in 0x0359A4 and stored through the 0x035FFC clamp. It is in the units
     of a term added to 1.0. Nothing connects it to a pressure. WRONG.
 A8  ImportAE5L600L.java:5458, :5460 "desc_evap_ect_lower/upper" (0xACF64,
     0xACF78); :434-436 "evap_ect_threshold_lookup ... EVAP ECT-based ...
     lower/upper EVAP pressure bounds"; diag_tasks_analysis.txt:255-258 "Reads
     ECT (FFFF63CC)".
     The lookup input is [0xFFFF63CC] at 0x06662E (and its copy [0xFFFF7920] at
     0x035458/0x035582), never 0xFFFF6350. The two tables are the lower/upper
     window widths of this controller ([78D4]/[78E0] in 0x0353BC) and 0x066626
     recomputes the same edges around [318C]. WRONG. (The "ECT" reading of
     0xFFFF63CC is one this project has already retracted.)
 A9  ImportAE5L600L.java:437-439 "evap_condition_eval ... Checks RPM,
     manifold_pressure, IAT, engine_run_time, purge flow".
     0x06665E has neither 0xFFFF6624 nor 0xFFFF620C in its pool. It copies [43FC],
     [7904], [40C8], [7D68], [78B8], [77DC] and byte [7912] and tests [7904]
     against the [318C]-based edges: it evaluates THIS controller. GBR =
     0xFFFF933C and the cal range are right. UNSUPPORTED / partly WRONG.
 A10 ImportAE5L600L.java:2103-2105 "desc_read_float_safe ... Reads float from
     descriptor ptr r4 ... Returns validated float or 0" (0x0BDBCC);
     cl_ol_analysis.txt:140 ("table utility"); fueling_pipeline_analysis.txt:
     1864 ("FFFF318C = descriptor").
     0x0BDBCC WRITES fr4 to [r4] and two checksum words to [r4+4], [r4+6]
     (0x0BDBF4, 0x0BDBF6, 0x0BDBFC) and returns 0, or 1 for a refused NaN. WRONG.
     (disassembly/analysis/helpers_desc_read_trace.txt:767, written the same
     day, already reads it as a record store.)
 A11 ImportAE5L600L.java:2155-2157 "desc_read_int_safe" (0x0BDCB6);
     cl_ol_analysis.txt:146-148 "If result != 0: R14 = 1 (valid)".
     0x0BDCB6 checks the float record's two checksum words; r0 = 1 means BOTH
     are wrong. So 0x0357F0 returns 1 for an INVALID record: the sense in
     cl_ol_analysis.txt is inverted. WRONG.
 A12 ImportAE5L600L.java:2124-2126 (0x0BE56C), second sentence: "Clamps fr4 to
     [fr6, fr5]. If fr4>fr5: fr7=fr5; elif fr4<fr6: fr7=fr6".
     Reversed. Bytes: not (fr4 > fr5) -> fr5; not (fr6 > fr4) -> fr6. fr5 is
     the LOWER bound, fr6 the UPPER. The first sentence of the same comment

################################################################################
REVIEW HISTORY  (third decode, 2026-11-27)
################################################################################
A tracer wrote this file and scripts/trace/emu_hole9_afl.py; an adversarial
reviewer disputed five claims and one contradiction and listed omissions.
Neither was presumed right. Each item was re-decided from bytes and, where
numeric, in the emulator. The lines below in instruction format are fn_dump
output and are checked by the verifier like every other line.

 C18  "the outer clamp binds only when the window itself lies beyond +-0.5"
      REVIEWER RIGHT, tracer wrong (and contradicted by the tracer's own rows).
      The clamp at 0x035734 changes the value exactly when inner is NaN, < -0.5
      or > +0.5. Emulated on 931 enabled cases with that predicate asserted: 100
      bind, 44 of them with a window that straddles zero (window -4.99..+5.01,
      total 1.99 -> 0.5); a NaN L gives -0.5. The "927 of 928" figure was a
      property of the tracer's grid (now 930 of 931, and labelled as such).
      The reviewer's own replacement wording ("the window must extend beyond
      +-0.5 AND the total must be out there too") is itself loose: with a
      window wholly beyond +-0.5 the inner clamp returns a window edge, so the
      outer clamp binds whatever the total is. The file states the condition
      on inner, which is what the bytes test (0x035726, 0x035734).
 C22  "[77C8]-[318C] >= 0.895 ... [63F8] >= 0.15 ... [6350] >= -40 ... [63D4] >= lookup"
      REVIEWER RIGHT. Every compare sense the tracer wrote is correct; the ">="
      wording is not, because three of the tests are "not (c > x)":
0350BA: F985  fcmp/gt fr8,fr9               ; T = (0.15 > [63F8]); the bf at 0x0350BC goes on when T = 0
0350CA: F985  fcmp/gt fr8,fr9               ; T = (-40 > [6350]); the bf at 0x0350CC goes on when T = 0
0350EE: F985  fcmp/gt fr8,fr9               ; T = (lookup > [63D4]); the bt at 0x0350F0 disables when T = 1
      Emulated at the exact thresholds and one float either side, and with a
      NaN in each input: NaN in [63F8], [6350] or [63D4] leaves the enable byte
      at 1; NaN in [6624] or [77C8] clears it. The tracer's grid stopped 1e-4
      short of the thresholds and had no NaN; section D2b replaces it.
 C24  "0x0359A4 runs on every call of 0x03580C"
      REVIEWER RIGHT. The tail jump and the call order are as the tracer said,
      but the body is gated:
035816: C432  mov.b @(50,gbr),r0            ; r0 = byte [0xFFFF7986]
03581C: 3022  cmp/hs r2,r0                  ; T = ([7986] >= byte [0xCBBEF] = 1), unsigned
03581E: 8B35  bf 0x03588C                   ; T = 0 -> the whole body, 0x0359A4 included, is skipped
      Emulated: entry value 0 skips (0x0359A4 not executed, [318C] keeps 0.02),
      the next call runs it. The tracer's assertion did not test this: its
      scenario always ran init first (which sets the byte to 0xFF), and it
      tested membership in an executed-set that is never cleared, so after the
      first pass it was vacuous. Fixed (the set is cleared per call, and
      section G4 enters with 0x00, 0x01, 0x02, 0x7F, 0x80 and 0xFF).
 C31  "0x06665E ... writes a single byte ... the only uses of the [7904] copy"
      REVIEWER RIGHT on both points. The function writes ten locations (write
      set asserted on 1,517 runs), and the copy is read twice more:
0668D2: 5241  mov.l @(4,r4),r2              ; 0x0668B4: r2 = [0x097520+4] = 0xFFFF9340
066922: 5241  mov.l @(4,r4),r2              ; 0x066904: the same slot
      Both functions are now listed, modelled and emulated (4,032 cases). The
      reviewer's readings of their conditions were checked and are correct.
      Added here beyond the review: 0x06665E reaches its two tests only when
      [43FC] is far from the setpoint copy (0x0667BA, 0x0667CC), and the other
      four loaders of the table (0x066D50, 0x066E1C, 0x066EBC, 0x066F94) were
      checked not to use +4: the code they reach addresses the table at +40 ..
      +60 only (asserted mechanically in emu section E2e).
 C49  "stock [7904] stays within about -0.13..+0.09" (basis: inferred)
      BOTH PARTLY RIGHT; now proven instead of inferred. The numbers hold, but
      only after init, as the reviewer said: 0x035E88 maps every stored L into
      [-0.028, +0.038], and before it runs only the +-0.5 clamps bound a stored
      L. The reviewer's "up to 0.01 in one call" is confirmed (an L between 0.05
      and 0.06 meeting a +-0.05 site). The reviewer's remaining gap -- "the
      conditions that select the step sites are still untraced" -- is closed:
      0x0359A4 is decoded in full (PART 5.3) and emulated against a model on
      5,022 states; a step needs a 156-call period. The reviewer's "normal move
      0.0005 per call" is an upper bound per call, not a rate: with the vote
      counter restarting at 512, at most one step happens in any 157 calls
      (emulated: steps on calls 156 and 313 with a constant bias). The bound
      also assumes finite inputs: a NaN in L gives -0.5, and so can a NaN
      window width.
 A3   open-holes.md title "an A/F-correction term" filed as UNSUPPORTED
      BOTH RIGHT IN PART. The tracer is right that the bytes do not establish
      the identity; the reviewer is right that nothing in them contradicts it
      and that there is supporting evidence the finding left out (the 0..5
      scaling of the input, the percent-shaped export, the twin's definition
      name). A3 keeps the verdict UNSUPPORTED, now marked "not contradicted",
      with that evidence listed. One caution neither party had: the supporting
      definition "AF 3 Correction Limits" is a project-only entry with no
      counterpart in 32BITBASE.xml, so it is weaker support than a base
      definition would be.

Omissions the reviewer listed, each checked before it went in:
  * second writer of 0xFFFF77BC..77C8 (0x0332A2)        confirmed; listed, emulated (E1b)
  * readers of 0xFFFF7944 in 0x035DE4 / 0x035E2C          confirmed; listed, emulated (E3)
  * writer of 0xFFFF43FC (0x00C5FC, u16 * 5/65536)        confirmed; listed, emulated (J)
  * "a second store form at 0x00C482"                     NOT a store: it is the low half
                                                          of the pool literal at 0x00C480
  * the gate's NaN behaviour missing from the claims      now E11
  * byte [7986] == 0 skips 0x0359A4                       confirmed; now E12

File and script problems the reviewer reported, and what was done:
  * "find_writers FFFF790C" does not show the phantom     true; E10 reworded
  * new_emu() zeroed 0xFFFF7700..0xFFFF7A00                removed. Every run now sets its
                                                          inputs explicitly; section D1b
                                                          uses the strictness to list the
                                                          state init does not write (E20)
  * calibration equality asserted for three ranges only,  section 0 now asserts twenty ranges
    the 19 bins never checked                             and reads all 19 bins
  * gate grid 1e-4 off the thresholds                     section D2b
  * "runs on every call" / "writes ONE byte" /            corrected in PART 3, 5.3, 5.4, 6
    "the only uses of the copy"
Not changed: every instruction line of the first version verified then and
verifies now; no compare sense, operand or address in the original listings
needed correcting.

ROUNDING MODE (2026-11-27, after corrections item 106)
The emulator used to round every single-precision result to nearest; the chip
rounds toward zero (reset loads FPSCR = 0x00040001; the ROM self-test at
0x058696 demands truncated results). The script's models had been written with
Python float arithmetic (round-to-nearest) and stopped matching. Every model
fadd/fsub/fmul/fdiv/fmac is now the exact rational result truncated once to
float32 (fmac fused), and every comparison that was bit-exact is still
bit-exact. What changed in the script:
  * the models (lpf 0x0BEA40, band test 0x0BE608, 0x0355B8, the siblings, the
    gate's d and reload, 0x034F28, 0x035E88, 0x033460, the scalers 0x0BE5A8 /
    0x0BE5D8 -- whose "+ 0.5" had been done in double -- 0x06665E, 0x0668B4 /
    0x066904, 0x0359A4, the section I window bound) use the toward-zero ops;
  * expectations that were round-to-nearest constants are now computed by the
    models: "[7904] after init = f32(0.02) + f32(0.01)" (three checks in
    section H) and "init pulls 0.3 in to f32(0.038)" (section I2);
  * the window-edge probes of E2c/E2d are built with the toward-zero fadd.
No contract, count or classification changed: the same 1,535 / 931 / 930 /
100 (44, 39, 16, 1) / 5,022 / 4,032 / 1,517 figures come out. Two printed totals
in the PART 3 table moved in the sixth decimal (0.593345 -> 0.593344,
0.553345 -> 0.553344). The 0x035E88 rewrite targets are 0x3D1BA5E3
(0.0379999988, the same float as nearest) and 0xBCE56040 (-0.0279999971, two
floats toward zero from the nearest float to -0.028); "0.038" / "-0.028" in the
text are those values to the precision shown. The table lookups are still
compared with an independent double-precision interpolation at the stated
tolerances (1e-7 .. 2e-6); 0x0BE830 itself is not modelled here.
